2026-11-03T03:38:02.5522172Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 25 with the greedy fusion on the real vocabulary and record its serving dispatch with the score

The empty commit 21dbaaf carried this run's message but changed no
file, so the push path filter skipped it. This commit adds the change
that the round-22 failure called for: right after score.json exists,
the sbatch keeps each Decode rank's once-per-process greedy decision
(the fused-call line with rows, vocab and stride, or the fallback
reason) in results/decode-greedy-dispatch.txt, and records explicitly
when no such line was logged. A test pins its place between the score
check and the acceptance collection.

The run itself is as 21dbaaf describes. Round 24's Decode trace showed
the round-22 greedy verify fusion never ran in serving because its
patch, graph buffer, wrapper check and gate assumed a 152064-token
vocabulary while the model has 151936. 4b40ae4 through 094995b take the
width from the model config and the logits shape, keep the CUDA kernel
unchanged, make the Decode gate read the model's config.json, and log
the fallback reason or the first fused call once per process. CPU
evidence covers both widths, B1 to B48 and edge values; CUDA 13.4 SASS
of every other kernel is unchanged. The image's CUDA is 13.0.88, so this
builds fresh. Everything else is main 38b1e6a.

Expected: 0.12-0.16 ms per Decode step now in serving; Prefill still
sets throughput, so the score change should be small.

Validation: the full suite under CPython 3.13 exits 0 and ruff passes.
